4202: function esc(value = '') {
4203:   return String(value).replace(/[&<>'"]/g, (char) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', "'": '&#39;', '"': '&quot;' }[char]));
4204: }

5790: async function recoverWorkshopToolImage(image) {
5791:   if (!(image instanceof HTMLImageElement) || image.dataset.recoveryRunning === '1') return;
5792:   image.dataset.recoveryRunning = '1';
5793:   const shell = image.closest('[data-workshop-tool-image-shell]');
5794:   shell?.classList.remove('has-image-error');
5795:   const fallbackSource = String(image.dataset.fallbackSrc || '');
5796:   const candidates = [];
5797:   if (fallbackSource) {
5798:     candidates.push(fallbackSource);
5799:     try {
5800:       const freshUrl = new URL(fallbackSource, document.baseURI);
5801:       freshUrl.searchParams.set('tool-image-recovery', `${VERSION}-${Date.now()}`);
5802:       candidates.push(freshUrl.href);
5803:     } catch (_) {}
5804:   }
5805:   for (const candidate of [...new Set(candidates.filter(Boolean))]) {
5806:     try {
5807:       const response = await fetch(candidate, { cache: 'reload', credentials: 'same-origin' });
5808:       if (!response.ok) continue;
5809:       const blob = await response.blob();
5810:       if (!String(blob.type || '').toLowerCase().startsWith('image/')) continue;
5811:       const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
5812:       image.addEventListener('load', () => URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl), { once: true });
5813:       image.src = objectUrl;
5814:       image.dataset.recovered = '1';
5815:       image.dataset.recoveryRunning = '0';

10823: async function loadStorytellerQuestions() {
10824:   if (Array.isArray(storytellerQuizQuestions) && storytellerQuizQuestions.length) return storytellerQuizQuestions;
10825:   if (!storytellerQuizLoadPromise) {
10826:     storytellerQuizLoadPromise = fetch(`${STORYTELLER_QUIZ_URL}?v=${VERSION}`, { cache: 'no-store' })
10827:       .then((response) => { if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`); return response.json(); })
10828:       .then((payload) => {
10829:         const rows = Array.isArray(payload?.questions) ? payload.questions : [];
10830:         storytellerQuizQuestions = rows.filter((row) => row && row.question && row.choices && ['A','B','C','D'].every((key) => String(row.choices[key] || '').trim()) && ['A','B','C','D'].includes(row.correct));
10831:         return storytellerQuizQuestions;
10832:       }).finally(() => { storytellerQuizLoadPromise = null; });
10833:   }
10834:   return storytellerQuizLoadPromise;
10835: }

11309: function shuffleEventAttempts(attempts) {
11310:   const shuffled = [...attempts];
11311:   for (let index = shuffled.length - 1; index > 0; index -= 1) {
11312:     const swapIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * (index + 1));
11313:     [shuffled[index], shuffled[swapIndex]] = [shuffled[swapIndex], shuffled[index]];
11314:   }
11315:   return shuffled;
11316: }
11317: 
11318: function tryRandomEventStarters(starters) {
11319:   for (const start of shuffleEventAttempts(starters)) {
11320:     if (start()) return true;
11321:   }
11322:   return false;
11323: }
11324: 
11325: function tryRandomNamedEventStarters(entries) {
11326:   for (const entry of shuffleEventAttempts(entries)) {
11327:     if (entry?.start?.()) return String(entry.id || 'event');
11328:   }
11329:   return '';
11330: }

11309: function shuffleEventAttempts(attempts) {
11310:   const shuffled = [...attempts];
11311:   for (let index = shuffled.length - 1; index > 0; index -= 1) {
11312:     const swapIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * (index + 1));
11313:     [shuffled[index], shuffled[swapIndex]] = [shuffled[swapIndex], shuffled[index]];
11314:   }
11315:   return shuffled;
11316: }

11319:   for (const start of shuffleEventAttempts(starters)) {
11320:     if (start()) return true;
11321:   }
11322:   return false;
11323: }

11515: async function tryRandomOkachimachiEntryEvent(eventState) {
11516:   // v0.10.799: 御徒町イベントは固定優先順を廃止し、外部から入るたびに判定順をシャッフルする。

11518:   const attempts = [
11519:     () => maybeStartOkachimachiInvasiveTurtlesEvent(),
11520:     () => maybeStartWristFoundEvent(),
11521:     () => maybeStartPandaMusicEvent(),
11522:     () => maybeStartOkachimachiTollEvent(),

11527:     () => maybeStartSpeedStarEvent(),
11528:     () => maybeStartStorytellerEvent(),
11529:     () => maybeStartRetroBattleEvent(),
11530:   ];

11532:     attempts.push(() => startOkachimachiQuizIfDue(eventState));
11533:   }
11534:   for (const attempt of shuffleEventAttempts(attempts)) {
11535:     if (await attempt()) return true;

11537:   return false;

14652: function startCinemaVisitPlayback() {
14653:   const video = root.querySelector('video[data-cinema-event-video]');

15035: function chooseMysteryChineseMealImage(previous = '') {
15036:   const images = [...MYSTERY_CHINESE_MEAL_EVENT_IMAGES];
15037:   if (!images.length) return '';
15038:   if (images.length === 1) return images[0];
15039:   const filtered = images.filter((name) => name !== previous);
15040:   return filtered[Math.floor(Math.random() * filtered.length)] || images[Math.floor(Math.random() * images.length)] || images[0];
15041: }

15058:   const stage = video.closest('.mystery-chinese-meal-video-stage');
15059:   stage?.classList.remove('needs-start', 'has-error');
15060:   video.muted = false;
15061:   video.volume = 1;
15062:   const playPromise = video.play();
15063:   if (playPromise && typeof playPromise.catch === 'function') {
15064:     playPromise
15065:       .then(() => {
15066:         stage?.classList.add('is-playing');
15067:         stage?.classList.remove('needs-start', 'has-error');
15068:       })
15069:       .catch(() => stage?.classList.add('needs-start'));
15070:   }
15071: }

15622: function grayHoodAquariumEventState() {
15623:   state.events = state.events && typeof state.events === 'object' && !Array.isArray(state.events) ? state.events : {};
15624:   const saved = state.events.grayHoodAquariumEvent && typeof state.events.grayHoodAquariumEvent === 'object' && !Array.isArray(state.events.grayHoodAquariumEvent)
15625:     ? state.events.grayHoodAquariumEvent : {};
15626:   const dialogueStages = new Set(['intro1', 'intro2', 'intro2b', 'intro3', 'intro3b', 'reward', 'farewell', 'unlockMessage']);
15627:   const validStages = new Set(['idle', 'video', ...dialogueStages, 'completed']);
15628:   const rawStage = validStages.has(saved.stage) ? saved.stage : 'idle';
15629:   const rawResumeStage = dialogueStages.has(saved.stageAfterVideo) ? saved.stageAfterVideo : '';
15630:   Object.assign(saved, {
15631:     active: Boolean(saved.active),
15632:     completed: Boolean(saved.completed),
15633:     stage: rawStage,
15634:     triggeredDay: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(saved.triggeredDay) || 0)),
15635:     introVideoCompleted: Boolean(saved.introVideoCompleted),
15636:     stageAfterVideo: rawResumeStage,
15637:   });
15638:   if (saved.completed) {
15639:     saved.active = false;
15640:     saved.stage = 'completed';
15641:     saved.introVideoCompleted = true;
15642:     saved.stageAfterVideo = '';
15643:   } else if (saved.active && !saved.introVideoCompleted) {
15644:     // v0.10.579より前にイベント途中だったセーブでも、重要な導入動画を飛ばさず
15645:     // 視聴後に元の進行段階へ正確に戻す。
15646:     if (saved.stage !== 'video') saved.stageAfterVideo = dialogueStages.has(saved.stage) ? saved.stage : (saved.stageAfterVideo || 'intro1');
15647:     if (!saved.stageAfterVideo) saved.stageAfterVideo = 'intro1';
15648:     saved.stage = 'video';
15649:   }
15650:   state.events.grayHoodAquariumEvent = saved;
15651:   return saved;
--- lines 15641-15690 ---

15658: function startGrayHoodAquariumIntroPlayback() {
15659:   const e = grayHoodAquariumEventState();
15660:   if (!e.active || e.stage !== 'video') return;
15661:   const video = root.querySelector('video[data-gray-hood-aquarium-intro-video]');
15662:   if (!(video instanceof HTMLVideoElement)) return;
15663:   const stage = video.closest('.gray-hood-aquarium-video-stage');
15664:   stage?.classList.remove('needs-start', 'has-error');
15665:   video.muted = false;
15666:   video.volume = 1;
15667:   suspendAudio();
15668:   const playPromise = video.play();
15669:   if (playPromise && typeof playPromise.catch === 'function') {
15670:     playPromise
15671:       .then(() => {
15672:         stage?.classList.add('is-playing');
15673:         stage?.classList.remove('needs-start', 'has-error');
15674:       })
15675:       .catch(() => stage?.classList.add('needs-start'));
15676:   }
15677: }

15684:   startGrayHoodAquariumIntroPlayback();

17153: function renderSupplier() {
--- lines 17407-17456 ---
17407:   const cost = Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(item?.cost) || 0));
17408:   if (!cost) return 0;

17412:   return Math.max(cost + 1000, roundThousand(cost * multiplier));
17413: }

20110: function giftVisualMarkup(payload, className = 'gift-item-visual') {

20114:   if (payload.type === 'metal') return metalVisual(payload.id, className, payload.name || '地金');

20117:     return item?.image

20124:   }
20125:   return `<span class="${className}" aria-hidden="true">◆</span>`;
20126: }

20666: function usePhoneItem(itemId) {

21017: async function copyGameDataForAI() {
21018:   const text = buildAIConsultationText();
21019:   let copied = false;
21020:   try {
21021:     if (navigator.clipboard?.writeText) {
21022:       await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
21023:       copied = true;
21024:     }
21025:   } catch (_) {
21026:     copied = false;
21027:   }
21028:   if (!copied) copied = fallbackCopyText(text);
21029:   if (!copied) {
21030:     showToast('ゲームデータをコピーできませんでした。ブラウザのクリップボード許可を確認してください。', 'error');
21031:     return;
21032:   }
21033:   playSfx('success');
21034:   showToast('AI相談用の簡易データをコピーしました。', 'info', false);
21035: }

21167: function buildSaveDiagnosticsSnapshot() {
21168:   const snapshot = structuredClone(state || {});
21169:   // v0.10.771: 内部診断も実セーブと同じ整理後サイズを測る。実stateは変更しない。
21170:   compactLongTermHistory(snapshot);
21171:   compactFinanceHistory(snapshot);
21172:   snapshot.saveRevision = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(snapshot.saveRevision) || 0)) + 1;
21173:   snapshot.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
21174:   if (snapshot.inventory && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(snapshot.inventory, 'general')) delete snapshot.inventory.general;
21175:   if (snapshot.inventory && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(snapshot.inventory, 'gems')) delete snapshot.inventory.gems;
21176:   snapshot.migrations = snapshot.migrations && typeof snapshot.migrations === 'object' && !Array.isArray(snapshot.migrations) ? snapshot.migrations : {};
21177:   snapshot.migrations.looseInventoryCanonicalV231 = true;
21178:   snapshot.saveSchemaVersion = SAVE_SCHEMA_VERSION;
21179:   const raw = JSON.stringify(snapshot);
21180:   return {
21181:     bytes: new TextEncoder().encode(raw).length,
21182:     nextSaveRevision: snapshot.saveRevision,
21183:     archivedClosedOrders: Array.isArray(snapshot.history?.closedOrders) ? snapshot.history.closedOrders.length : 0,
21184:     archivedSoldJewelry: Array.isArray(snapshot.history?.soldJewelry) ? snapshot.history.soldJewelry.length : 0,
21185:   };
21186: }

21188: function saveDiagnosticsCapacityLabel(projectedCount, maxCount) {
21189:   if (maxCount <= 0) return '確認不能';
21190:   if (projectedCount > maxCount) return 'クラウド上限超過';
21191:   if (projectedCount === maxCount) return '上限付近';
21192:   if (projectedCount >= Math.ceil(maxCount * 0.8)) return '注意';
21193:   return '余裕あり';
21194: }

21315:   const salesRows = [];

21317:   if (looseSoldCount > 0) salesRows.push(`<div><span>ルース売却</span><strong>${looseSoldCount}個</strong></div>`);
21318:   if (sold.length) salesRows.push(`<div><span>ジュエリー販売</span><strong>${sold.length}点</strong></div>`);
21319:   salesRows.push(`<div><span>来店人数</span><strong>${Math.max(0, Number(result.visitors) || 0)}人</strong></div>`);

21322:   const balance = income - expense;
21323:   const balancePrefix = balance > 0 ? '＋' : balance < 0 ? '－' : '';
21324:   const balanceClass = balance > 0 ? 'positive' : balance < 0 ? 'negative' : 'neutral';
21325:   const meals = Array.isArray(result.meals) ? result.meals : [];
